TL;DR: LOFT是一个低秩正交微调框架，明确分离了适应子空间和该子空间内的变换，通过将正交适应视为乘法子空间旋转，统一了多种正交PEFT方法，并提出基于梯度信息的任务感知支持选择策略，在多个任务上提升了效率-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交参数高效微调方法混淆了适应发生的子空间与子空间内应用的变换这两个独立的设计选择，缺乏对支持选择（support selection）这一核心设计维度的系统认识。

Method: 提出LOFT框架，将正交适应形式化为乘法子空间旋转，显式分离子空间和变换；通过一阶分析表明有效的适应支持应受下游训练信号指导，从而设计任务感知的支持选择策略。

Result: 在语言理解、视觉迁移、数学推理和多语言分布外适应任务中，LOFT恢复了主子空间正交适应，梯度信息引导的支持选择在匹配参数量、内存和计算预算下改善了效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 原则性的支持选择是改进正交参数高效微调的重要方向。

Abstract: Orthogonal parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts pretrained weights through structure-preserving multiplicative transformations, but existing methods often conflate two distinct design choices: the subspace in which adaptation occurs and the transformation applied within that subspace. This paper introduces LOFT, a low-rank orthogonal fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates these two components. By viewing orthogonal adaptation as a multiplicative subspace rotation, LOFT provides a unified formulation that recovers representative orthogonal PEFT methods, including coordinate-, butterfly-, Householder-, and principal-subspace-based variants. More importantly, this perspective exposes support selection as a central design axis rather than a byproduct of a particular parameterization. We develop a first-order analysis showing that useful adaptation supports should be informed by the downstream training signal, motivating practical task-aware support selection strategies. Across language understanding, visual transfer, mathematical reasoning, and multilingual out-of-distribution adaptation, LOFT recovers principal-subspace orthogonal adaptation while gradient-informed supports improve the efficiency-performance trade-off under matched parameter, memory, and compute budgets. These results suggest that principled support selection is an important direction for improving orthogonal PEFT.

</details>


### [111] [Sobolev Regularized MMD Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11884)
*Chenyang Tian,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur,Arthur Gretton,Zonghao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Sobolev正则化的最大均值差异梯度流（SrMMD），通过在证人函数上施加梯度惩罚来缓解MMD目标的非凸性，从而在连续和离散时间下均能获得MMD的全局收敛保证，且无需目标分布的等周假设，同时适用于采样和生成建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MMD梯度流面临目标函数非凸性导致的收敛困难问题，且现有收敛分析通常依赖目标分布的等周假设；此外，以往的梯度流方法通常仅适用于生成建模或采样中的一种，而非两者兼用。

Method: 在MMD的证人函数上引入Sobolev范数（梯度罚项）作为正则化项，得到SrMMD梯度流；收敛分析基于核均值嵌入差分的正则性条件，而非等周假设；在采样设置中使用Stein核。

Result: 在连续时间和离散时间下均证明了MMD的全局收敛性；该方法在生成建模和采样任务上均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: SrMMD梯度流是一种兼具理论保证和广泛适用性的方法，统一了采样与生成建模的梯度流框架。

Abstract: We propose Sobolev-regularized Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SrMMD) gradient flow, a regularized variant of maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) gradient flow based on a gradient penalty on the witness function. The proposed regularization mitigates the non-convexity of the MMD objective and yields provable \emph{global} convergence guarantees in MMD in both continuous and discrete time. A more surprising appeal is that our convergence analysis does not rely on isoperimetric assumptions on the target distribution. Instead, it is based on a regularity condition on the difference between kernel mean embeddings. A key highlight of the proposed flow is that it is applicable in both sampling (from an unnormalized target distribution) -- using Stein kernels -- and generative modeling settings, unlike previous works, where a gradient flow is suitable for only generative modeling or sampling but not both. The effectiveness of the proposed flow is empirically verified on a broad range of tasks in both generative modelling and sampling.

</details>


### [112] [Incentivizing Truthfulness and Collaborative Fairness in Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11889)
*Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Jue Fan,Xiao Tian,Xinyi Xu,Patrick Jaillet,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对贝叶斯模型的机制，结合半值（如Shapley值）和基于验证集的诚实数据估值函数，同时保证协作公平性并激励数据真实性，理论分析和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法无法验证或激励数据真实性，参与者可能通过提交重复或噪声数据人为提高估值和奖励，或阻止他人获益，需要一种能同时保证公平和激励诚实的机制。

Method: 结合半值（如Shapley值）保证协作公平性，引入基于验证集（对参与者未知）的诚实数据估值函数，并增加额外条件证明参与者可通过提交真实知识的数据最大化其期望数据值和半值。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上验证了理论结果，并讨论了中介预算有限或缺乏验证集时的松弛条件。

Conclusion: 该机制是首个在贝叶斯模型中同时保证协作公平性和激励真实性的方案，为联邦学习等场景提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Collaborative machine learning involves training high-quality models using datasets from a number of sources. To incentivize sources to share data, existing data valuation methods fairly reward each source based on its data submitted as is. However, as these methods do not verify nor incentivize data truthfulness, the sources can manipulate their data (e.g., by submitting duplicated or noisy data) to artificially increase their valuations and rewards or prevent others from benefiting. This paper presents the first mechanism that provably ensures (F) collaborative fairness and incentivizes (T) truthfulness at equilibrium for Bayesian models. Our mechanism combines semivalues (e.g., Shapley value), which ensure fairness, and a truthful data valuation function (DVF) based on a validation set that is unknown to the sources. As semivalues are influenced by others' data, we introduce an additional condition to prove that a source can maximize its expected data values in coalitions and semivalues by submitting a dataset that captures its true knowledge. Additionally, we discuss the implications and suitable relaxations of (F) and (T) when the mediator has a limited budget for rewards or lacks a validation set. Our theoretical findings are validated on synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [113] [Procedural-skill SFT across capacity tiers: A W-Shaped pre-SFT Trajectory and Regime-Asymmetric Mechanism on 0.8B-4B Qwen3.5 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11907)
*Igor Strozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Qwen3.5系列（0.8B/2B/4B）上测量了过程性技能SFT的贡献，发现SFT带来的提升在不同规模上大致均匀（约+0.04~+0.075），但后SFT差异主要由前SFT基线的W型轨迹主导；同时揭示了基准格式一致性伪影和负迭代序列对绝对通过率上限的约束，并通过GPT-5.4交叉验证了结果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量过程性技能SFT在不同规模模型上的实际贡献，澄清此前研究中因路径不匹配导致的伪影（如"0.8B仅格式提升"和"4B上SFT效果递减"的错误结论）。

Method: 在200任务/40技能的保留集上，使用353条（任务+过程性技能块, Opus链式推理, 裁判通过）演示数据进行SFT；采用匹配路径的LLM-only评分，并引入GPT-5.4进行跨族系交叉验证（Cohen's κ≥0.754）。

Result: SFT带来的过程性Δ提升在0.8B/2B/4B上分别为+0.070/+0.040/+0.075，均匀分布；后SFTΔ（-0.005/+0.100/+0.065）主要由前SFT基线的W型轨迹（-0.075/+0.060/-0.010，Haiku-4.5为+0.030）主导；发现基准格式一致性伪影（83.5%保留集使用确定性ANSWER行提取器，低估自由形式结论）和负迭代序列（0.8B上五种配方变体后SFT通过率在2pp范围内波动）。

Conclusion: SFT在模型对过程性技能表现困难时贡献最大，呈现"区间不对称"模式，并提出了可在8B/14B上验证的预测；此前"0.8B仅格式提升"和"4B上SFT效果递减"的结论是路径不匹配伪影，本文予以推翻。

Abstract: We measure procedural-skill SFT contribution across three Qwen3.5 dense scales (0.8B, 2B, 4B) on a 200-task / 40-skill holdout, with Claude Haiku 4.5 as a frontier reference. The corpus is 353 rows of (task + procedural-skill block, Opus chain-of-thought, judge-pass) demonstrations.
  \textbf{Main finding.} Under matched-path LLM-only scoring, the SFT-attributable procedural-$Δ$ lift is roughly uniform across sizes: $+0.070$ / $+0.040$ / $+0.075$ at 0.8B / 2B / 4B. Variation in post-SFT $Δ$ ($-0.005$, $+0.100$, $+0.065$) is dominated by a W-shaped pre-SFT base trajectory ($-0.075$, $+0.060$, $-0.010$, Haiku-4-5 at $+0.030$): the 5-step procedure hurts 0.8B and 4B, helps 2B, and helps frontier Haiku modestly. SFT works hardest in absolute terms where the base struggles with the procedure -- a regime-asymmetric pattern with a falsifiable prediction at 8B/14B.
  \textbf{Methodology.} (i) A bench format-compliance artifact: 83.5\% of the holdout uses a deterministic \texttt{ANSWER}-line extractor that under-counts free-form conclusions; an LLM-only re-judge reveals it was systematically biased against \CU. (ii) A negative-iteration sequence at 0.8B: five recipe variants cluster post-SFT \CU{} pass-rate within a 2\,pp band, constraining the absolute-pass-rate ceiling to base capacity rather than recipe.
  \textbf{Cross-family validation.} GPT-5.4 via OpenRouter on all 7 configurations (2800 paired episodes) agrees on the direction of every per-student finding: Cohen's $κ\geq 0.754$, agreement $\geq 93.25\%$.
  Earlier ``format-only at 0.8B'' and ``shrinking SFT at 4B'' framings were path-mismatch artifacts; this paper supersedes both (Appendix~\ref{sec:appendix-path}). Single-seed; threats in §\ref{sec:threats}.

</details>


### [114] [Delightful Gradients Accelerate Corner Escape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11908)
*Jincheng Mei,Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Delightful Policy Gradient (DG)方法，通过将策略梯度项乘以优势与动作惊讶度的乘积，消除了策略在次优角点附近的自我陷阱机制。在K臂赌博机中证明零温极限下逃逸界为对数形式，在表格MDP中达到O(1/t)渐近收敛率，但在共享函数近似下可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Softmax策略梯度虽然在渐近意义下以O(1/t)收敛，但在单纯形次优角点附近存在指数级慢的瞬态行为，其根源是自我陷阱机制：负优势动作会强化角点策略，初始阶段可能反向推动最优动作。

Method: 提出Delightful Policy Gradient (DG)，其核心是对每个策略梯度项乘以优势与动作惊讶度（surprisal）的乘积，从而在次优角点附近定量消除自我陷阱机制。理论分析揭示了一个关键结构洞见：任何优于角点动作的动作都是"盟友"，其对逃逸的贡献非负。

Result: 在K臂赌博机中，零温极限下DG的首次逃逸界与初始概率比呈对数关系；在固定温度下，有害动作随其变罕见而被多项式压制。在赌博机和表格MDP中，DG全局收敛到最优策略，渐近收敛率为O(1/t。通过精确反例证明该机制在共享函数近似下可能失效，但在MNIST上下文赌博机实验中DG仍能从坏初始化中更快恢复。

Conclusion: DG有效解决了Softmax策略梯度的自我陷阱问题，在表格设置中保证快速全局收敛，但共享函数近似下的失效边界标记了理论的适用范围而非实际限制。

Abstract: Softmax policy gradient converges at $O(1/t)$, but its transient behavior near sub-optimal corners of the simplex can be exponentially slow. The bottleneck is self-trapping: negative-advantage actions reinforce the corner policy and can initially push the optimal action backward. We study \emph{Delightful Policy Gradient} (DG), which gates each policy-gradient term by the product of advantage and action surprisal. For $K$-armed bandits, we prove that the zero-temperature limit of DG removes this corner-trapping mechanism on a quantitative sector near any sub-optimal corner, yielding a first-exit escape bound logarithmic in the initial probability ratio. At every fixed temperature, the same local mechanism persists because harmful actions are polynomially suppressed as they become rare. A key structural insight is that every action better than the corner action is an \emph{ally}: its contribution to escape is non-negative. Combining corner instability with a monotonic value improvement identity, we prove that DG converges globally to the optimal policy in both bandits and tabular MDPs at an asymptotic $O(1/t)$ rate. We also show, via an exact counterexample, that this tabular mechanism can fail under shared function approximation. In MNIST contextual bandits with a shared-parameter neural network, DG nevertheless recovers from bad initializations faster than standard policy gradient, suggesting that the counterexample marks a boundary of the theory rather than a practical prohibition.

</details>


### [115] [STAGE: Tackling Semantic Drift in Multimodal Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11919)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Xunkai Li,Yihan Sun,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为STAGE的协议优先框架，用于解决多模态联邦图学习中客户端间语义空间不一致的问题，通过先构建共享语义空间再规范表示传播的方式实现跨客户端语义校准，在多种任务上取得了最优性能并降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）面临客户端来自不同模态领域导致语义空间不共享的挑战，直接参数协调不可靠，且会引发虚假语义一致性和图消息传递放大残差不一致两个下游问题。

Method: 提出STAGE协议优先框架，不依赖直接参数平均，而是先构建共享语义空间将异构多模态特征转换为可比较的表示，再规范这些表示在局部图结构上的传播方式。

Result: 在8个多模态属性图上的5种图中心与模态中心任务中，STAGE一致取得最优性能，同时降低了每轮通信负载。

Conclusion: STAGE通过协议优先的方式有效解决了MM-FGL中的跨客户端语义校准问题，并减少了不一致性在图学习中放大的风险。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables collaborative training on graph data across multiple clients. As graph data increasingly contain multimodal node attributes such as text and images, multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) has become an important yet substantially harder setting. The key challenge is that clients from different modality domains may not share a common semantic space: even for the same concept, their local encoders can produce inconsistent representations before collaboration begins. This makes direct parameter coordination unreliable and further causes two downstream problems: forcing heterogeneous client representations into a naively shared semantic space may create false semantic agreement, and graph message passing may amplify residual inconsistency across neighborhoods. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{STAGE}, a protocol-first framework for MM-FGL. Instead of relying on direct parameter averaging, STAGE builds a shared semantic space that first translates heterogeneous multimodal features into comparable representations and then regulates how these representations propagate over local graph structures. In this way, STAGE not only improves cross-client semantic calibration, but also reduces the risk of inconsistency amplification during graph learning. Extensive experiments on 8 multimodal-attributed graphs across 5 graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that STAGE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing per-round communication payload.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Order Fairness: Mitigating LLMs Order Sensitivity through Dual Group Advantage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11974)
*Xu Chu,Guanyu Wang,Zhijie Tan,Xinrong Chen,Ziyu Li,Tong Mo,Weiping Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双组优势优化（DGAO），首次使用强化学习来缓解大语言模型的顺序敏感性问题，同时提升模型准确性和顺序稳定性。实验表明DGAO在RAG、数学推理和分类任务上实现了更优的顺序公平性和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs存在顺序偏差（order bias），即输入元素的排列顺序会影响模型性能，这种不公平性限制了模型在上下文学习和检索增强生成等场景中的应用。现有方法如基于统计结果或数据集搜索获取最优排列会增加推理开销且无法解决固有偏差，而通过多顺序变体增强集进行监督微调又往往以牺牲准确性为代价，使模型陷入一致但不正确的幻觉。

Method: 提出DGAO（双组优势优化）框架，计算并平衡组内相对准确率优势（intra-group relative accuracy advantage）和组间相对稳定性优势（inter-group relative stability advantage），对生成顺序稳定且正确输出的策略模型给予奖励，对顺序敏感或错误响应给予惩罚。这是首次将强化学习用于缓解LLM的顺序敏感性。同时提出两个新指标Consistency Rate和Overconfidence Rate来揭示先前方法的伪稳定性。

Result: 大量实验表明，DGAO在RAG、数学推理和分类任务上实现了更优的顺序公平性，同时提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: DGAO通过强化学习范式有效解决了LLM的顺序敏感性问题，在保证顺序公平性的同时不牺牲准确性，为未来相关工作提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) suffer from order bias, where their performance is affected by the arrangement order of input elements. This unfairness limits the model's applications in scenarios such as in-context learning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Recent studies attempt to obtain optimal or suboptimal arrangements based on statistical results or using dataset-based search, but these methods increase inference overhead while leaving the model's inherent order bias unresolved. Other studies mitigate order sensitivity through supervised fine-tuning using augmented training sets with multiple order variants, but often at the cost of accuracy, trapping the model in consistent yet incorrect hallucinations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{D}ual \textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}dvantage \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{DGAO}), which aims to improve model accuracy and order stability simultaneously. DGAO calculates and balances intra-group relative accuracy advantage and inter-group relative stability advantage, rewarding the policy model for generating order-stable and correct outputs while penalizing order-sensitive or incorrect responses. This marks the first time reinforcement learning has been used to mitigate LLMs' order sensitivity. We also propose two new metrics, Consistency Rate and Overconfidence Rate, to reveal the pseudo-stability of previous methods and guide more comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGAO achieves superior order fairness while improving performance on RAG, mathematical reasoning, and classification tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hyalinesky/DGAO.

</details>


### [117] [Stochastic Minimum-Cost Reach-Avoid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11975)
*Jingduo Pan,Taoran Wu,Yiling Xue,Bai Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于"reach-avoid概率证书"(RAPCs)的随机最小代价可达避免强化学习方法，通过压缩型Bellman公式将概率约束与成本优化结合，在保证高满足率的同时优化代价，并证明了算法几乎必然收敛到局部最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全和约束强化学习方法在随机环境中难以同时满足概率性可达避免约束并优化成本，缺乏有效的机制来联合处理这两个目标。

Method: 提出reach-avoid概率证书(RAPCs)识别可满足约束的状态；在此基础上构建基于压缩的Bellman公式作为替代目标，将可达避免约束融入强化学习，实现概率约束下的代价优化。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真器上的实验表明，所提算法在成本性能和可达避免满足率两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提算法几乎必然收敛到局部最优策略，为随机环境中概率约束下的代价优化强化学习提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We study stochastic minimum-cost reach-avoid reinforcement learning, where an agent must satisfy a reach-avoid specification with probability at least $p$ while minimizing expected cumulative costs in stochastic environments. Existing safe and constrained reinforcement learning methods typically fail to jointly enforce probabilistic reach-avoid constraints and optimize cost in the learning setting in stochastic environments. To address this challenge, we introduce reach-avoid probability certificates (RAPCs), which identify states from which stochastic reach-avoid constraints are satisfiable. Building on RAPCs, we develop a contraction-based Bellman formulation that serves as a principled surrogate for integrating reach-avoid considerations into reinforcement learning, enabling cost optimization under probabilistic constraints. We establish almost sure convergence of the proposed algorithms to locally optimal policies with respect to the resulting objective. Experiments in the MuJoCo simulator demonstrate improved cost performance and consistently higher reach-avoid satisfaction rates.

</details>


### [118] [QDSB: Quantized Diffusion Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11983)
*Tobias Fuchs,Florian Kalinke,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化扩散薛定谔桥（QDSB），通过锚点量化端点分布来加速生成模型训练，在保持样本质量的同时显著减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无模拟薛定谔桥（SB）模型需要配对数据，而获取全局最优耦合在实际中不可行，局部小批量求解熵最优传输问题不仅重复计算成本高，还会扭曲全局传输几何结构。因此需要一种更高效的方法来加速训练并保持全局传输特性。

Method: QDSB方法首先对源和目标端点分布进行锚点量化，在量化后的分布上计算端点耦合（求解熵最优传输问题），然后通过逐单元采样将得到的传输计划提升回原始数据点。

Result: 理论证明正则化最优耦合对锚点量化具有稳定性，误差由锚点近似的质量控制。在真实世界实验中，QDSB在匹配现有基线样本质量的同时，所需时间大幅减少。

Conclusion: QDSB通过锚点量化策略有效解决了SB模型中耦合计算的计算瓶颈，在保证理论稳定性的前提下实现了显著的加速效果。

Abstract: Learning generative models in settings where the source and target distributions are only specified through unpaired samples is gaining in importance. Here, one frequently-used model are Schrödinger bridges (SB), which represent the most likely evolution between both endpoint distributions. To accelerate training, simulation-free SBs avoid the path simulation of the original SB models. However, learning simulation-free SBs requires paired data; a coupling of the source and target samples is obtained as the solution of the entropic optimal transport (OT) problem. As obtaining the optimal global coupling is infeasible in many practical cases, the entropic OT problem is iteratively solved on minibatches instead. Still, the repeated cost remains substantial and the locality can distort the global transport geometry. We propose quantized diffusion Schrödinger bridges (QDSB), which compute the endpoint coupling on anchor-quantized endpoint distributions and lift the resulting plan back to original data points through cell-wise sampling. We show that the regularized optimal coupling is stable w.r.t. anchor quantization, with an error controlled by the quality of the anchor approximation. In real-world experiments, QDSB matches the sample quality of existing baselines, requiring substantially less time. Code and data are available at github.com/mathefuchs/qdsb.

</details>


### [119] [Split the Differences, Pool the Rest: Provably Efficient Multi-Objective Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12000)
*Ziyad Sheebaelhamd,Luca Viano,Volkan Cevher,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-BC算法，通过分区处理冲突专家数据并聚合无冲突状态-动作对，在MOMDP中恢复Pareto最优策略，理论上证明其比独立处理专家数据具有更快的收敛速度且达到极小化最优，实验验证了离散和连续环境下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法无法处理多目标场景中冲突的专家轨迹，直接聚合会导致被支配策略，因此需要一种能从多个Pareto最优专家中恢复Pareto前沿策略的方法。

Method: 提出Multi-Output Augmented Behavioral Cloning (MA-BC)算法，系统地对存在分歧的专家数据进行分区，同时聚合无行为冲突的状态-动作对，在MOMDP框架下恢复Pareto最优策略。

Result: 理论证明了MA-BC比独立处理每个专家数据集具有更快的统计收敛速度，并建立了多目标模仿学习的下界，证明MA-BC达到极小化最优；在离散环境和连续LQR控制任务中进行了实证验证。

Conclusion: MA-BC有效解决了多目标模仿学习中的专家冲突问题，在理论和实证层面均表现出优越性，为MOMDP中的策略恢复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This work investigates multi-objective imitation learning: the problem of recovering policies that lie on the Pareto front given demonstrations from multiple Pareto-optimal experts in a Multi-Objective Markov Decision Process (MOMDP). Standard imitation approaches are ill-equipped for this regime, as naively aggregating conflicting expert trajectories can result in dominated policies. To address this, we introduce Multi-Output Augmented Behavioral Cloning (MA-BC), an algorithm that systematically partitions divergent expert data while pooling state-action pairs where no behavior conflict is observed. Theoretically, we prove that MA-BC converges to Pareto-optimal policies at a faster statistical rate than any learner that considers each expert dataset independently. Furthermore, we establish a novel lower bound for multi-objective imitation learning, demonstrating that MA-BC is minimax optimal. Finally, we empirically validate our algorithm across diverse discrete environments and, guided by our theoretical insights, extend and evaluate MA-BC on a continuous Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) control task.

</details>


### [120] [Limits of Learning Linear Dynamics from Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12010)
*Aybüke Ulusarslan,Niki Kilbertus,Nora Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究受控线性时不变(LTI)系统在非完全可辨识条件下的可辨识性边界，证明实验设置（初始状态和控制输入）决定了可恢复信息的基本极限，并发现即使整个系统不可辨识，实验可达子空间上的受限动力学仍可被唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据驱动方法隐式假设系统可辨识，但实际中该假设常不成立，导致错误预测和无效的机理性结论。经典理论仅给出充分条件，未解决条件不满足时哪些部分仍可辨识的问题。

Method: 建立几何特征化方法来描述实验设置所决定的可恢复信息基本极限，推导出与实验设置一致的所有系统的闭式描述。

Result: 即使整个系统不可辨识，实验可达子空间上的受限动力学仍可被唯一确定。

Conclusion: 揭示了实验设置本身决定了可辨识性的基本极限，并给出了部分可辨识性的精确刻画。

Abstract: Learning governing dynamics from data is a common goal across the sciences, yet it is only well-posed when the underlying mechanisms are identifiable. In practice, many data-driven methods implicitly assume identifiability; when this assumption fails, estimated models can yield spurious predictions and invalid mechanistic conclusions. Classical identifiability guarantees for controlled linear time-invariant (LTI) systems provide sufficient conditions -- controllability and persistent excitation -- but leave open whether identifiability holds when these conditions fail, and which parts of the system remain identifiable without full identifiability. We show that the experimental setup, i.e., the realized initial state and control input, dictates a fundamental limit on the information recoverable from the observed trajectory. We develop a geometric characterization of this limit and derive a closed-form description of all systems consistent with the experimental setup. Crucially, we prove that even when the full system is not identifiable, the restricted dynamics on the subspace reachable by the experiment remain uniquely determined.

</details>


### [121] [Resilient Vision-Tabular Multimodal Learning under Modality Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12031)
*Camillo Maria Caruso,Valerio Guarrasi,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态Transformer框架，通过可学习的模态令牌、掩码自注意力机制和模态丢弃正则化策略，在无需数据插补或模型切换的情况下，有效处理医学影像和表格数据中模态和特征同时缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态深度学习方法通常假设模态完全可用，但实际临床中常出现整个模态或个体特征缺失的情况，亟需一种能稳健处理普遍性模态缺失的方法。

Method: 构建了三组件架构（视觉编码器、表格编码器、多模态融合编码器），通过可学习模态令牌加权单模态表示，采用带掩码自注意力的中间融合机制排除缺失标记；引入模态丢弃正则化策略，在训练时随机移除可用模态以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR和MIMIC-IV数据集上进行14种诊断发现的多标签分类，通过渐进式缺失压力测试，该方法在所有缺失场景下持续优于基线方法，性能下降更平滑且鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 注意力层掩码和带联合微调的中间融合是实现稳健多模态推理的关键，该方法有效应对了临床场景中的模态缺失挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal deep learning has shown strong potential in medical applications by integrating heterogeneous data sources such as medical images and structured clinical variables. However, most existing approaches implicitly assume complete modality availability, an assumption that rarely holds in real-world clinical settings where entire modalities and individual features are frequently missing. In this work, we propose a multimodal transformer framework for joint vision-tabular learning explicitly designed to operate under pervasive modality missingness, without relying on imputation or heuristic model switching. The architecture integrates three components: a vision, a tabular, and a multimodal fusion encoder. Unimodal representations are weighted through learnable modality tokens and fused via intermediate fusion with masked self-attention, which excludes missing tokens and modalities from information aggregation and gradient propagation. To further enhance resilience, we introduce a modality-dropout regularization strategy that stochastically removes available modalities during training, encouraging the model to exploit complementary information under partial data availability. We evaluate our approach on the MIMIC-CXR dataset paired with structured clinical data from MIMIC-IV for multilabel classification of 14 diagnostic findings with incomplete annotations. Two parallel systematic stress-test protocols progressively increase training and inference missingness in each modality separately, spanning fully multimodal to fully unimodal scenarios. Across all missingness regimes, the proposed method consistently outperforms representative baselines, showing smoother performance degradation and improved robustness. Ablation studies further demonstrate that attention-level masking and intermediate fusion with joint fine-tuning are key to resilient multimodal inference.

</details>


### [122] [Learning plug-in surrogate endpoints for randomized experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12051)
*Alessandro-Umberto Margueritte,Ahmet Zahid Balcıoğlu,Jesse Krijthe,Dave Zachariah,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何在随机实验中学习可替代长期结果的“插件式复合替代终点”，提出了两种最大化效应预测能力的方法，并在合成和真实数据实验中验证了其优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期结果在随机实验中往往成本高昂或不可行，需要寻找短期替代终点。现有因果形式化标准大多不可识别，无法转化为实用的学习算法。

Method: 提出两种学习“插件式复合替代终点”的方法，最大化效应预测能力（effect predictiveness）；并分析了在何种场景下能找到产生无偏效应估计的替代终点。

Result: 在合成实验（已知真实效应）和真实实验数据中，基于直接建模替代效应的方法比已有方法返回的插件式终点更具预测性。

Conclusion: 直接建模替代效应的方法能更有效地学习插件式复合替代终点，为在随机实验中替代长期结果提供了实用算法。

Abstract: Surrogate endpoints are used in place of long-term outcomes in randomized experiments when observing the real outcome for a large enough cohort is prohibitively expensive or impractical. A short-term surrogate is good if the result of an experiment using the surrogate is predictive of the result of a hypothetical study using the real outcome. Much attention has been paid to formalizing this property in causal terms, but most criteria are unidentifiable and cannot be turned into practical algorithms for learning surrogate endpoints from data. To address this, we study plug-in composite surrogates, functions of post-treatment variables that may be substituted directly for the primary outcome in a randomized experiment. We propose two methods for learning plug-in surrogates that maximize effect predictiveness, and characterize the possibility of finding endpoints that yield unbiased effect estimates in representative scenarios. Finally, in both synthetic experiments with known effects and in data from a real-world experiment, we find that our method, based on directly modeling the surrogate effect, returns plug-in endpoints more predictive of the primary effect than established methods.

</details>


### [123] [Hölder Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12058)
*Yuxiang Chen,Dingli Liang,Yihang Chen,Ziqin Gong,Chenyang Le,Zhaokai Wang,Jiachen Zhu,Lingyu Yang,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HölderPO，通过Hölder均值统一token级概率聚合，并利用动态退火算法调节参数p以平衡梯度集中性与方差界限，在数学推理和具身任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖固定聚合机制将轨迹级优势映射到策略更新，导致训练崩溃与性能不足的固有权衡，缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出HölderPO框架，使用Hölder均值统一token概率聚合，通过参数p连续控制梯度集中性与方差界限的权衡，并设计动态退火算法在训练过程中渐进调度p值。

Result: 数学基准平均准确率54.9%，相对GRPO提升7.2%；ALFWorld成功率93.8%，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: HölderPO通过动态调节聚合机制有效解决了GRPO中固定聚合的适应性不足问题，实现了更优的稳定性与收敛性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) enhances large language models by estimating advantages across a group of sampled trajectories. However, mapping these trajectory-level advantages to policy updates requires aggregating token-level probabilities within each sequence. Relying on a fixed aggregation mechanism for this step fundamentally limits the algorithm's adaptability. Empirically, we observe a critical trade-off: certain fixed aggregations frequently suffer from training collapse, while others fail to yield satisfactory performance. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{HölderPO}, a generalised policy optimisation framework unifying token-level probability aggregation via the Hölder mean. By explicitly modulating the parameter $p$, our framework provides continuous control over the trade-off between gradient concentration and variance bounds. Theoretically, we prove that a larger $p$ concentrates the gradient to amplify sparse learning signals, whereas a smaller $p$ strictly bounds gradient variance. Because no static configuration can universally resolve this concentration-stability trade-off, we instantiate the framework with a dynamic annealing algorithm that progressively schedules $p$ across the training lifecycle. Extensive evaluations demonstrate superior stability and convergence over existing baselines. Specifically, our approach achieves a state-of-the-art average accuracy of $54.9\%$ across multiple mathematical benchmarks, yielding a substantial $7.2\%$ relative gain over standard GRPO and secures an exceptional $93.8\%$ success rate on ALFWorld.

</details>


### [124] [Missing Old Logits in Asynchronous Agentic RL: Semantic Mismatch and Repair Methods for Off-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12070)
*Zhong Guan,Yongjian Guo,Haoran Sun,Wen Huang,Shuai Di,Xiong Jun Wu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 异步强化学习中PPO风格的重要比率应分解为训练-推理差异项和策略陈旧项，但旧logits缺失问题破坏了这种解耦校正的语义。本文提出了三种精确获取旧logits的策略和一种近似校正方法（PPO-EWMA），在训练速度和优化性能上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习在提升大语言模型智能体吞吐量的同时，引入了PPO风格离策略校正的关键失败模式：旧logits缺失导致训练-推理差异校正与策略陈旧校正纠缠，破坏了原有的解耦语义，并使裁剪和掩码阈值产生不良交互。

Method: 1) 分析异步流水线中延迟更新和部分 rollout 导致旧 logits 缺失的问题；2) 提出三种精确获取旧logits的策略：快照版本追踪、专用旧logits模型、部分rollout中断同步；3) 从近似校正角度提出PPO-EWMA方法，在无法低成本恢复精确旧logits时采用更合适的近似策略。

Result: 采用PPO-EWMA方法在训练速度和优化性能上均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 异步PPO训练中需解耦处理训练-推理差异项和策略陈旧项，PPO-EWMA是兼顾系统开销与校正效果的实用方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning improves rollout throughput for large language model agents by decoupling sample generation from policy optimization, but it also introduces a critical failure mode for PPO-style off-policy correction. In heterogeneous training systems, the total importance ratio should ideally be decomposed into two semantically distinct factors: a \emph{training--inference discrepancy term} that aligns inference-side and training-side distributions at the same behavior-policy version, and a \emph{policy-staleness term} that constrains the update from the historical policy to the current policy. We show that practical asynchronous pipelines with delayed updates and partial rollouts often lose the required historical training-side logits, or old logits. This missing-old-logit problem entangles discrepancy repair with staleness correction, breaks the intended semantics of decoupled correction, and makes clipping and masking thresholds interact undesirably. To address this issue, we study both exact and approximate correction routes. We propose three exact old-logit acquisition strategies: snapshot-based version tracking, a dedicated old-logit model, and synchronization via partial rollout interruption, and compare their system trade-offs. From the perspective of approximate correction, we focus on preserving the benefits of decoupled correction through a more appropriate approximate policy when exact old logits cannot be recovered at low cost, without incurring extra system overhead. Following this analysis, we adopt a revised PPO-EWMA method, which achieves significant gains in both training speed and optimization performance. Code at https://github.com/millioniron/ROLL.

</details>


### [125] [Elicitation-Augmented Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12079)
*Alvar Haltia,Ville Hyvönen,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协同贝叶斯优化方法，通过成对比较（而非精确量化）来利用人类专家的隐性知识，并设计了成本感知的信息价值采集函数，在直接观测与成对查询之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HITL BO方法要求专家能够量化其知识（如标出查询位置或指定最大值先验分布），但人类专长往往是隐性的（tacit），难以明确量化。因此需要一种能在专家仅提供成对比较的情况下利用其知识的BO方法。

Method: (1) 将专家的成对比较判断解释为目标函数值的含噪证据；(2) 开发了一个结合直接观测和成对查询信息的原理性方法；(3) 推导了一个成本感知的信息价值（value-of-information）采集函数，用于在直接观测和成对查询之间进行平衡。

Result: 该方法趋近于个体信息源轨迹的凸包：当成对查询成本低时，显著提高样本效率；当成对查询成本高或噪声大时，能恢复到标准BO的性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够灵活适应不同成本和噪声水平的成对查询，在保持鲁棒性的同时有效利用人类隐性知识提升优化效率。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop Bayesian optimization (HITL BO) methods utilize human expertise to improve the sample-efficiency of BO. Most HITL BO methods assume that a domain expert can quantify their knowledge, for instance by pinpointing query locations or specifying their prior beliefs about the location of the maximum as a probability distribution. However, since human expertise is often tacit and cannot be explicitly quantified, we consider a setting where domain knowledge of an expert is elicited via pairwise comparisons of designs. We interpret the expert's pairwise judgements as noisy evidence about the values of the observable objective function and develop a principled method for combining the information obtained via direct observations and pairwise queries. Specifically, we derive a cost-aware value-of-information acquisition function that balances direct observations against pairwise queries. The proposed method approaches the convex hull of the trajectories of the individual information sources: when pairwise queries are cheap it substantially improves sample-efficiency over observation-only BO, and when pairwise queries are costly or noisy, it recovers the performance of standard BO by relying on direct observations alone.

</details>


### [126] [Disentangled Sparse Representations for Concept-Separated Diffusion Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12122)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Hangyeol Jung,Heechan Yun,Sungjun Yun,Dong-Jun Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAEParate方法，通过概念感知对比目标和GeLU增强编码器，在文本到图像扩散模型的概念遗忘中实现更精确的特定概念抑制，在UnlearnCanvas上达到SOTA，尤其在风格-物体联合遗忘任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的遗忘方法因稀疏重建目标未显式强制概念级分离，导致潜在特征在不同概念间共享，造成目标概念抑制不精确且产生非目标干扰。

Method: 1) 提出概念感知对比目标（concept-aware contrastive objective），将潜在表示组织成概念特定的聚类；2) 增强编码器引入GeLU-based非线性变换，提升表达容量，实现更具判别性和解耦性的潜在空间。

Result: 在UnlearnCanvas基准上达到SOTA性能，尤其在联合风格-物体遗忘这一具有挑战性的场景中，显著优于现有方法，有效减少了目标与非目标概念间的干扰。

Conclusion: 通过显式概念分离和增强编码器表达能力的SAEParate方法，能够在无需修改模型参数的情况下，实现更精确的概念抑制和更少的意外干扰，为扩散模型的安全可控生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Unlearning specific concepts in text-to-image diffusion models has become increasingly important for preventing undesirable content generation. Among prior approaches, sparse autoencoder (SAE)-based methods have attracted attention due to their ability to suppress target concepts through lightweight manipulation of latent features, without modifying model parameters. However, SAEs trained with sparse reconstruction objectives do not explicitly enforce concept-wise separation, resulting in shared latent features across concepts. To address this, we propose SAEParate, which organizes latent representations into concept-specific clusters via a concept-aware contrastive objective, enabling more precise concept suppression while reducing unintended interference during unlearning. In addition, we enhance the encoder with a GeLU-based nonlinear transformation to increase its expressive capacity under this separation objective, enabling a more discriminative and disentangled latent space. Experiments on UnlearnCanvas demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains in joint style-object unlearning, a challenging setting where existing methods suffer from severe interference between target and non-target concepts.

</details>


### [127] [Fused Gromov-Wasserstein Distance with Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12161)
*Harlin Lee,Ying Yu,Mingxin Li,Ranthony Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出带特征选择的融合Gromov-Wasserstein距离，通过自适应特征抑制权重增强高维场景下的可解释性与鲁棒性，并开发了交替最小化算法，在计算选区划分等任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FGW距离对所有特征统一处理，在高维场景下大量无关或噪声特征会降低可解释性与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出两种特征选择机制：(1) 基于Lasso和Ridge惩罚的正则化FGW；(2) 基于单纯形约束权重的FGW（含分组扩展）。设计了交替最小化算法进行求解。

Result: 建立了与经典FGW和GW距离的边界关系及度量性质的理论保证；实验表明特征抑制增强了可解释性并揭示了任务相关结构。

Conclusion: 特征选择的FGW距离在保持对齐能力的同时，通过自适应降权无关特征提升了模型的解释性和鲁棒性，计算选区划分等实际应用验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) distances provide a principled framework for comparing objects by jointly aligning structure and node features. However, existing FGW formulations treat all features uniformly, which limits interpretability and robustness in high-dimensional settings where many features may be irrelevant or noisy. We introduce FGW distances with feature selection, which incorporate adaptive feature suppression weights into the FGW objective to selectively downweight or suppress differentiating features during alignment. We propose two approaches: (1) regularized FGW with Lasso and Ridge penalties, and (2) FGW with simplex-constrained weights, including groupwise extensions. We analyze the resulting models and establish their key theoretical properties, including bounds relative to classical FGW and Gromov-Wasserstein distances, and metric behavior. An efficient alternating minimization algorithm is developed. Experiments illustrate how feature suppression enhances interpretability and reveals task-relevant structure, with a special application to computational redistricting.

</details>


### [128] [On What We Can Learn from Low-Resolution Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12168)
*Theresa Dahl Frehr,Niels Henrik Pontoppidan,Hiba Nassar,Tommy Sonne Alstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验角度分析了在数据分辨率混合场景下，低分辨率数据对高分辨率模型训练的贡献，证明了当高分辨率数据稀缺时，加入低分辨率数据能持续提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中（如医疗、公共机构、可穿戴设备），数据常因存储、隐私或资源限制而无法以高分辨率集中收集，导致数据集由混合分辨率样本组成。现有研究未充分回答低分辨率数据对高分辨率模型评估的贡献程度，该问题缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 基于KL散度进行理论分析，刻画数据点的影响如何随分辨率变化，并推导出上下界关联高低分辨率观测的相对贡献与下采样造成的信息损失。在视觉Transformer和CNN上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论推导了分辨率变化下数据点影响力的变化规律及信息损失边界；实验证明在两种视觉模型上，高分辨率数据稀缺时加入低分辨率数据可稳定提升性能。

Conclusion: 低分辨率数据在混合分辨率训练场景中具有实用价值，能够弥补高分辨率数据不足带来的性能损失，为该类常见但缺乏理论指导的实际问题提供了理论依据和实验支持。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems typically rely on large, centrally collected datasets, a premise that does not hold in many real-world domains such as healthcare and public institutions. In these settings, data sharing is often constrained by storage, privacy, or resource limitations. For example, small wearable devices may lack the bandwidth or energy capacity needed to store and transmit high-resolution data, leading to aggregation during data collection and thus a loss of information. As a result, datasets collected from different sources may consist of a mixture of high- and low-resolution samples. Despite the prevalence of this setting, it remains unclear how informative low-resolution data is when models are ultimately evaluated on high-resolution inputs. We provide a theoretical analysis based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that characterises how the influence of a datapoint changes with resolution, and derive bounds that relate the relative contribution of high- and low-resolution observations to the information lost under downsampling. To support this analysis, we empirically demonstrate, using both a vision transformer and a convolutional neural network, that adding low-resolution data to the training set consistently improves performance when high-resolution data is scarce.

</details>


### [129] [Expected Batch Optimal Transport Plans and Consequences for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12174)
*Samuel Boïté,Julie Delon,Kimia Nadjahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了流匹配中随机小批量最优传输的期望耦合$\overlineπ_{k}$，建立了大批量一致性，在半离散情况下推导了传输成本偏差和耦合收敛到OT计划的速率，并量化了OT批量大小与数值积分之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中使用小批量OT作为精确OT的替代，但其在总体层面诱导的耦合性质尚未被充分理解，本文旨在形式化并分析这一期望耦合。

Method: 将期望批OT计划$\overlineπ_{k}$定义为独立小批量上经验OT计划的平均；建立大批量一致性；在半离散情形下推导传输成本偏差和耦合收敛速率；通过两原子模型及合成/图像实验量化OT批量大小与数值积分的交互。

Result: 建立了$\overlineπ_{k}$的大批量一致性；推导了半离散情形下的传输成本偏差和收敛速率；证明了该耦合诱导的速度场足够正则以定义从源到离散目标的唯一流；量化了OT批量大小与数值积分误差的交互关系。

Conclusion: 小批量OT在流匹配中产生的期望耦合具有良好的大样本性质和正则性，批量大小的选择需要在传输成本偏差和数值积分成本之间权衡。

Abstract: Solving optimal transport (OT) on random minibatches is a common surrogate for exact OT in large-scale learning. In flow matching (FM), this surrogate is used to obtain OT-like couplings that can straighten probability paths and reduce numerical integration cost. Yet, the population-level coupling induced by repeated minibatch OT remains only partially understood. We formalize this coupling as the expected batch OT plan $\overlineπ_{k}$, obtained by averaging empirical OT plans over independent minibatches of size $k$. We then establish its large-batch consistency and, in the semidiscrete case relevant to generative modeling, derive rates for both the transport-cost bias and the convergence of $\overlineπ_{k}$ to the OT plan. For FM, this yields a population coupling whose induced velocity field is regular enough to define a unique flow from the source to the discrete target. We finally quantify how OT batch size interacts with numerical integration in a tractable two-atom model and in synthetic and image experiments.

</details>


### [130] [Multi-Task Representation Learning for Conservative Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12176)
*Jiabin Lin,Shana Moothedath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了约束多任务表征学习（CMTRL）框架，用于解决线性bandit问题。该框架考虑T个共享低维表示（维度r）的线性bandit任务，且每个任务需满足安全或性能约束。作者提出了Safe-AltGDmin算法来恢复低秩特征矩阵，并在此基础上构建了多任务表征学习框架，给出了遗憾界和样本复杂度的理论保证，并通过实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性bandit研究多关注单任务或未考虑安全约束的多任务场景，缺乏同时处理多任务共享低维表示和任务级安全/性能约束的方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一个统一的框架来兼顾表征共享和保守性约束。

Method: 提出Safe-AltGDmin算法，通过交替投影梯度下降和最小化来恢复低秩特征矩阵，同时满足约束条件。在此基础上构建CMTRL框架，将多任务保守线性bandit问题与低秩表征学习相结合。

Result: 建立了算法的遗憾界和样本复杂度理论保证，并通过实验与基准算法对比，验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: CMTRL框架成功地将保守约束引入多任务线性bandit表征学习，Safe-AltGDmin算法在理论上可证明有效，实验也支持了其优越性。

Abstract: This paper presents the Constrained Multi-Task Representation Learning (CMTRL) framework for linear bandits. We consider T linear bandit tasks in a d dimensional space, which share a common low-dimensional representation of dimension r, where r is much smaller than the minimum of d and T. Furthermore, tasks are constrained so that only actions meeting specific safety or performance requirements are allowed, referred to as conservative (safe) bandits. We introduce a novel algorithm, Safe-Alternating projected Gradient Descent and minimization (Safe-AltGDmin), to recover a low-rank feature matrix while satisfying the given constraints. Building on this algorithm, we propose a multi-task representation learning framework for conservative linear bandits and establish theoretical guarantees for its regret and sample complexity bounds. We presented experiments and compared the performance of our algorithm with benchmark algorithms.

</details>


### [131] [DriftXpress: Faster Drifting Models via Projected RKHS Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12183)
*Ali Falahati,Elliot Creager,Gautam Kamath,Shubhankar Mohapatra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DriftXpress，一种基于投影RKHS域的加速漂移模型公式，通过低秩特征空间近似漂移核，在保持单步推理优势的同时降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型通过将迭代去噪过程替换为单次生成器评估实现单步生成，但将大量计算从推理转移到训练中，导致训练成本高昂。本文旨在进一步推动这种训练-推理权衡，在保持单步推理优势的前提下降低训练开销。

Method: 基于投影RKHS（再生核希尔伯特空间）域，在低秩特征空间中近似漂移核，保留原始漂移场的吸引-排斥结构，同时降低场评估的计算成本。

Result: 在图像生成基准测试中，DriftXpress在FID指标上与标准漂移模型相当，同时减少了墙钟训练时间。

Conclusion: 漂移模型的训练-推理权衡可以进一步优化，在不牺牲单步推理优势的前提下降低训练成本。

Abstract: Drifting Models have emerged as a new paradigm for one-step generative modeling, achieving strong image quality without iterative inference. The premise is to replace the iterative denoising process in diffusion models with a single evaluation of a generator. However, this creates a different trade-off: drifting reduces inference cost by moving much of the computation into training. We introduce DriftXpress, an accelerated formulation of drifting models based on projected RKHS fields. DriftXpress approximates the drifting kernel in a low-rank feature space. This preserves the attraction-repulsion structure of the original drifting field while reducing the cost of field evaluation. Across image-generation benchmarks, DriftXpress achieves comparable FID to standard drifting while reducing wall-clock training cost. These results show that the training-inference trade-off of drifting models can be pushed further without giving up their one-step inference advantage.

</details>


### [132] [Fair Conformal Classification via Learning Representation-Based Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12195)
*Senrong Xu,Yanke Zhou,Yuhao Tan,Zenan Li,Yuan Yao,Taolue Chen,Feng Xu,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分类任务的公平共形推断框架，通过构建在自适应识别子组上保证条件覆盖的预测集，平衡预测集的有效性与效率，并确保跨不公平处理子组的自适应均衡覆盖，从而促进可信机器学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测方法提供统计上严格的边际覆盖保证，但无法解释算法偏差，从而破坏了公平性和信任。现有方法缺乏对隐式非线性特征组合定义的子组的条件覆盖保证。

Method: 提出公平共形推断框架，构建在自适应识别子组（可通过非线性特征组合隐式定义）上保证条件覆盖的预测集，平衡预测集的紧凑性与信息性，并确保跨不公平处理子组的自适应均衡覆盖。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验证明了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为迈向可信机器学习提供了一条实用路径，在保证统计覆盖的同时解决了算法公平性问题。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods provide statistically rigorous marginal coverage guarantees for machine learning models, but such guarantees fail to account for algorithmic biases, thereby undermining fairness and trust. This paper introduces a fair conformal inference framework for classification tasks. The proposed method constructs prediction sets that guarantee conditional coverage on adaptively identified subgroups, which can be implicitly defined through nonlinear feature combinations. By balancing effectiveness and efficiency in producing compact, informative prediction sets and ensuring adaptive equalized coverage across unfairly treated subgroups, our approach paves a practical pathway toward trustworthy machine learning. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework.

</details>


### [133] [ECTO: Exogenous-Conditioned Temporal Operator for Ultra-Short-Term Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12196)
*Cao Yuan,Junjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECT0框架，通过物理引导变量选择(PGVS)和外部条件驱动状态细化(ECRR)两个模块实现超短期风电功率预测。该方法在不同气候、容量和外生维度的三个风电场中均取得最优MSE，相对最强基线提升2.2%-5.2%，长预测期提升达8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超短期风电功率预测对电网调度和备用管理至关重要，但由于风电的非平稳性和条件依赖性特征，预测面临挑战。气象外生变量携带重要预测信息，但最优变量组合随站点、运行条件和预测时域变化。现有深度学习方法要么将外生输入作为通用辅助通道处理，要么依赖PCA等固定预处理步骤，未能利用气象变量的物理结构。

Method: 提出ECT0框架，包含两个互补模块：(1)物理引导变量选择(PGVS)模块，利用领域物理先验和sparsemax激活函数进行分层、分组感知的稀疏变量选择，生成紧凑且条件自适应的外生上下文；(2)外部条件驱动状态细化(ECRR)模块，通过混合专家范式将预测路由至学习到的领域专家，应用增益-偏置校准和时域特定修正。

Result: 在三个不同气候、容量(66-200MW)和外生维度(11-13个变量)的风电场实验中，ECT0在所有站点取得最低MSE，相对最强基线提升2.2%-5.2%，在长预测期(H=32)提升达8.6%。消融分析显示PGVS贡献+1.84%，ECRR贡献+2.86%。

Conclusion: ECT0框架通过物理引导变量选择和外部条件驱动状态细化解耦设计，有效提升了超短期风电功率预测精度。PGVS能够学习到物理有意义、站点特定的变量选择模式，ECRR则收敛到跨站点一致且分离良好的校准策略，验证了该方法对外生变量物理结构利用的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate ultra-short-term wind power forecasting is critical for grid dispatch and reserve management, yet remains challenging due to the non-stationary, condition-dependent nature of wind generation. Meteorological exogenous variables carry substantial predictive information, but the most informative variable combination varies across sites, operating conditions, and prediction horizons. Existing deep learning approaches either treat exogenous inputs as generic auxiliary channels through uniform mixing or soft gating, or rely on fixed preprocessing steps such as PCA, without exploiting the physical structure of meteorological variables. We propose ECTO (Exogenous-Conditioned Temporal Operator), a unified framework that decomposes exogenous variable modeling into two complementary modules. Physically-Grounded Variable Selection (PGVS) performs hierarchical, group-aware sparse selection over exogenous variables using a domain-informed physical prior and sparsemax activations, producing a compact, condition-adaptive exogenous context. Exogenous-Conditioned Regime Refinement (ECRR) routes the forecast through learned regime experts that apply gain--bias calibration and horizon-specific corrections via a mixture-of-experts paradigm. Experiments on three wind farms spanning different climates, capacities (66--200 MW), and exogenous dimensions (11--13 variables) demonstrate that ECTO achieves the lowest MSE across all sites, with relative improvements over the strongest baseline ranging from 2.2% to 5.2%, widening to 8.6% at the longer prediction horizon ($H=32$). Ablation analysis confirms that each exogenous-related component contributes positively (PGVS +1.84%, ECRR +2.86%), and interpretability analysis reveals that PGVS learns physically meaningful, site-specific variable selection patterns, while ECRR converges to well-separated calibration strategies consistent across sites.

</details>


### [134] [A Unified Graph Language Model for Multi-Domain Multi-Task Graph Alignment Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12197)
*Haibo Chen,Xin Wang,Jiaheng Chao,Ling Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UniGraphLM，通过多领域多任务GNN编码器学习可泛化的图表示，并自适应地与LLM令牌空间对齐，解决现有图语言模型中跨领域跨任务的表示对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GLM使用GNN作为图编码器时，忽略了跨领域、跨任务对齐GNN编码表示与LLM令牌空间的问题，导致难以泛化到多样化的图数据。

Method: 提出UniGraphLM，包含多领域多任务GNN编码器学习与文本语义对齐的可泛化图表示，并通过自适应对齐策略将这些表示与LLM令牌空间对齐。

Result: 实现了跨领域多任务的图表示与LLM的有效对齐，增强了GLM在多样化图数据上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniGraphLM通过多领域多任务GNN编码和自适应对齐策略，解决了图语言模型中跨领域跨任务的表示对齐难题。

Abstract: Leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) as graph encoders and aligning the resulting representations with Large Language Models (LLMs) through alignment instruction tuning has become a mainstream paradigm for constructing Graph Language Models (GLMs), combining the generalization ability of LLMs with the structural modeling capacity of GNNs. However, existing GLMs that adopt GNNs as graph encoders largely overlook the problem of aligning GNN-encoded representations across domains and tasks with the LLM token space to obtain unified graph tokens, thereby limiting their ability to generalize across diverse graph data. To bridge this gap, we aim to incorporate a multi-domain, multi-task GNN encoder into GLMs and align its representations with LLMs to enable multi-domain, multi-task graph alignment instruction tuning. This alignment problem remains underexplored and poses two key challenges: 1) learning GNN-encoded representations that are simultaneously generalizable across domains and tasks and well aligned with textual semantics is difficult, due to substantial variations in graph structures, feature distributions, and supervision signals, together with the lack of textual-semantic alignment guidance in task-specific GNN training; 2) diverse graph data and task-specific instructions can exhibit different degrees of compatibility with the LLM token space during instruction tuning, leading to varying alignment difficulty and rendering a fixed alignment strategy suboptimal. To tackle these challenges, we propose UniGraphLM, a Unified Graph Language Model that incorporates a multi-domain, multi-task GNN encoder to learn generalizable graph representations aligned with textual semantics, and then adaptively aligns these representations with the LLM.

</details>


### [135] [Overtrained, Not Misaligned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12199)
*Joel Schreiber,Ariel Goldstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"涌现性失调"(EM)现象进行了迄今为止最全面的研究，发现EM在GPT-4o中可复现但在开源模型中仅17%出现，且与模型规模正相关；通过检查点分析揭示EM在任务收敛后晚期出现，因此早停和适当学习率选择可有效避免EM，将其从不可预见的风险重新定义为可避免的训练伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Betley等人(2025)首次发现的"涌现性失调"(EM)现象进行全面验证和深入分析，探究其在多种开源模型中的普遍性、产生机制及缓解方法。

Method: 在GPT-4o和12个开源模型（覆盖Llama、Qwen、DeepSeek、GPT-OSS四个系列，参数量8B-671B）上进行微调实验，评估超过100万条模型响应，使用多种随机种子；通过检查点级别分析微调过程中EM的动态变化，并在医学微调领域进行跨域验证。

Result: EM在GPT-4o中可复现，但在12个开源模型中仅2个(17%)表现出一致的EM；模型规模与EM易感性显著正相关；EM在训练后期、主任务接近收敛后才出现；早停可在保留93%任务性能的同时消除EM，适当学习率选择可进一步降低风险；在医学微调中规模-EM相关性更强(r=0.90)，早停在67%情况下可避免泛化到不真实性。

Conclusion: EM并非无法预见的微调风险，而是一种可避免的训练伪影；通过早停和适当学习率设置等恰当的训练实践，EM可以被有效避免。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM), where fine-tuning on a narrow task (like insecure code) causes broad misalignment across unrelated domains, was first demonstrated by Betley et al. (2025). We conduct the most comprehensive EM study to date, reproducing the original GPT-4o finding and expanding to 12 open-source models across 4 families (Llama, Qwen, DeepSeek, GPT-OSS) ranging from 8B to 671B parameters, evaluating over one million model responses with multiple random seeds. We find that EM replicates in GPT-4o but is far from universal: only 2 of 12 open-source models (17%) exhibit consistent EM across seeds, with a significant correlation between model size and EM susceptibility. Through checkpoint-level analysis during fine-tuning, we demonstrate that EM emerges late in training, distinct from and subsequent to near convergence of the primary task, suggesting EM emerges from continued training past task convergence. This yields practical mitigations: early stopping eliminates EM while retaining an average of 93% of task performance, and careful learning rate selection further minimizes risk. Cross-domain validation on medical fine-tuning confirms these patterns generalize: the size-EM correlation strengthens (r = 0.90), and overgeneralization to untruthfulness remains avoidable via early stopping in 67% of cases, though semantically proximate training domains produce less separable misalignment. As LLMs become increasingly integrated into real-world systems, fine-tuning and reinforcement learning remain the primary methods for adapting model behavior. Our findings demonstrate that with proper training practices, EM can be avoided, reframing it from an unforeseen fine-tuning risk to an avoidable training artifact.

</details>


### [136] [Investigating simple target-covariate relationships for Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12200)
*Gaspard Berthelier,Mariia Baranova,Andrei-Tiberiu Pantea,Etienne Le Naour,Adrien Petralia,Tahar Nabil,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验评估了Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS两个时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）对协变量的整合能力，发现TabPFN-TS在捕捉简单协变量-目标关系方面优于Chronos-2，尤其在短预测范围内，表明Chronos-2的强基准性能并不自动转化为对简单依赖关系的最优建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TSFMs在零样本设置中表现优异，且新架构（如Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS）声称可整合协变量，但其对协变量-目标关系的实际建模能力尚不明确，现有基准测试可能掩盖了模型在简单依赖关系上的缺陷。

Method: 设计基于简单目标-协变量关系的控制实验，通过可控的合成数据比较Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS在协变量整合方面的能力。

Result: TabPFN-TS在捕捉简单协变量-目标关系上优于Chronos-2，尤其在短预测范围内表现更显著；Chronos-2的强基准性能并未体现在对这类简单依赖关系的建模上。

Conclusion: 当前TSFMs在协变量整合能力上存在差异，强基准性能不等于对简单因果关系的准确建模，未来需要更精细的评估框架来指导模型设计。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently achieved state-of-the-art performance, often outperforming supervised models in zero-shot settings. Recent TSFM architectures, such as Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS, aim to integrate covariates. In this paper, we design controlled experiments based on simple target-covariate relationships to assess this integration capability. Our results show that TabPFN-TS captures these relationships more effectively than Chronos-2, especially for short horizons, suggesting that the strong benchmark performance of Chronos-2 does not automatically translate into optimal modeling of simple covariate-target dependencies.

</details>


### [137] [On the Importance of Multistability for Horizon Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12206)
*Asad Bakija,Florent De Geeter,Julien Brandoit,Pierre Sacré,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中的时间视野泛化问题，证明多稳态（multistability）是时间视野泛化的必要条件，而复杂任务还需要瞬态动力学（transient dynamics）。研究表明，当前可并行化的RNN架构（如状态空间模型和门控线性RNN）由于结构上呈单稳态，无法实现视野泛化，而传统非线性RNN虽能同时具备两种动力学特性但不可并行化。因此，设计同时具备多稳态和瞬态动力学的可并行化架构是扩展长视野强化学习的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在POMDP中，智能体需要依赖记忆（通常由RNN编码）来整合历史观测信息。长视野（long-horizon）POMDP中相关观测与最优动作之间间隔大量时间步，导致训练面临泛化差、样本效率低和探索成本高的问题。理想情况下，在短视野上训练的智能体应能保持任意长视野上的最优行为，但缺乏形式化框架来刻画何时可实现这一性质。

Method: 首先形式化定义了时间视野泛化（temporal horizon generalization）——策略对所有视野保持最优的性质，并推导了其充要条件。然后通过实验评估了非线性可并行RNN变体（如状态空间模型、门控线性RNN）实现该性质的能力。最后从动力学角度解释RNN行为与时间视野泛化的关联。

Result: 多稳态是时间视野泛化的必要条件，在简单任务中也是充分条件；复杂任务还需瞬态动力学。现代可并行化架构（状态空间模型和门控线性RNN）由于结构上被设计为单稳态（monostable），因此无法跨时间视野泛化。当前没有一种可并行化的RNN同时具备多稳态和瞬态动力学。

Conclusion: 多稳态和瞬态动力学是时间视野泛化两个必不可少且互补的动力学机制。当前可并行化RNN架构缺乏这两种特性的结合，设计同时具备多稳态和瞬态动力学的可并行化架构是实现可扩展长视野强化学习的关键方向。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL), agents acting in partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) must rely on memory, typically encoded in a recurrent neural network (RNN), to integrate information from past observations. Long-horizon POMDPs, in which the relevant observation and the optimal action are separated by many time steps (called the horizon), are particularly challenging: training suffers from poor generalization, severe sample inefficiency, and prohibitive exploration costs. Ideally, an agent trained on short horizons would retain optimal behavior at arbitrarily longer ones, but no formal framework currently characterizes when this is achievable. To fill this gap, we formalized temporal horizon generalization, the property that a policy remains optimal for all horizons, derived a necessary and sufficient condition for it, and experimentally evaluated the ability of nonlinear and parallelizable RNN variants to achieve it. This paper presents the resulting theoretical framework, the empirical evaluation, and the dynamical interpretation linking RNN behavior to temporal horizon generalization. Our analyses reveal that multistability is necessary for temporal horizon generalization and, in simple tasks, sufficient; more complex tasks further require transient dynamics. In contrast, modern parallelizable architectures, namely state space models and gated linear RNNs, are monostable by construction and consequently fail to generalize across temporal horizons. We conclude that multistability and transient dynamics are two essential and complementary dynamical regimes for horizon generalization, and that no current parallelizable RNN exhibits both. Designing parallelizable architectures that combine these regimes thus emerges as a key direction for scalable long-horizon RL.

</details>


### [138] [Not How Many, But Which: Parameter Placement in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12207)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Charles Lovering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究LoRA适配器中B矩阵的参数放置问题：在给定k个可训练参数预算下，选择哪些参数很重要。SFT下随机和知情子集表现相当，但GRPO下随机放置失败，而梯度知情放置可恢复标准LoRA精度。这种差异源于梯度结构：SFT梯度低秩且方向稳定，GRPO梯度高秩且跨步骤近似正交。评分方法可在10秒内、低于0.5%训练成本下识别关键参数。这些参数集中在残差流写入投影（V、O、Down），跨模型族和规模（1.5B-8B）稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在LoRA微调中，当可训练参数预算有限时，参数放置策略（即选择B矩阵中哪些元素进行训练）是否以及如何影响模型性能，特别是在不同训练范式（SFT与GRPO）下。

Method: 通过对比随机放置与梯度知情放置策略在SFT和GRPO两种训练范式下的表现，分析梯度结构差异（秩、方向稳定性），并提出基于梯度符号一致性的高效评分方法，在极短时间内（<10秒）和极低计算成本（<0.5%训练成本）下识别关键参数。

Result: SFT下随机和知情子集表现相当；GRPO下随机放置无法改善基础模型，梯度知情放置恢复标准LoRA精度。关键参数集中在残差流写入投影（V、O、Down），且该发现跨模型族和规模（1.5B-8B）稳定。

Conclusion: 参数放置策略的重要性依赖于训练范式，GRPO下需要梯度知情的参数选择，而所提评分方法高效可靠，揭示了LoRA微调中参数的有效性规律。

Abstract: We study the \textit{parameter placement problem}: given a fixed budget of $k$ trainable entries within the B matrix of a LoRA adapter (A frozen), does the choice of which $k$ matter? Under supervised fine-tuning, random and informed subsets achieve comparable performance. Under GRPO on base models, random placement fails to improve over the base model, while gradient-informed placement recovers standard LoRA accuracy. This regime dependence traces to gradient structure: SFT gradients are low-rank and directionally stable, so any subset accumulates coherent updates; GRPO gradients are high-rank and near-orthogonal across steps, so only elements with consistently signed gradients retain the learning signal. Our scoring procedure identifies these critical parameters in under 10 seconds at less than 0.5% of training cost. Selected parameters concentrate on residual-stream-writing projections (V, O, Down), stable across model families and scales (1.5B - 8B).

</details>


### [139] [Intrinsic Vicarious Conditioning for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12224)
*Rodney A Sanchez,Ferat Sahin,Alex Ororbia,Jamison Heard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“替代性条件作用”（Vicarious Conditioning）的内在奖励机制，通过记忆-based方法实现注意、保持、再现和强化四个步骤，使智能体能够从观察他人行为中学习，无需访问演示者的策略或奖励函数。在MiniWorld Sidewalk和Box2D CarRacing环境中的实验表明，该方法能有效延长回合长度，避免非描述性终止状态，适用于小样本学习和持续学习场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习中的内在驱动力大多采用直接条件作用范式，限制了智能体从环境和他人中学习的能力。离线策略或示例学习方法虽能从演示者表征中学习，但需要访问演示者的策略或奖励函数，这在实际应用中往往不可行。

Method: 借鉴心理学和生物学中的替代性条件作用理论，通过记忆方法实现四个步骤：注意（观察他人行为）、保持（存储观察信息）、再现（模拟观察到的行为）、强化（将替代性信号转化为内在奖励）。该方法无需演示者的策略或奖励函数，支持小样本学习。

Result: 在MiniWorld Sidewalk环境（具有非描述性终止条件）和Box2D CarRacing环境中的实验表明，替代性条件作用能够有效延长智能体的回合长度，避免非描述性终止状态，并引导智能体朝向理想状态移动。

Conclusion: 该工作模拟了一种认知上更合理的学习范式，特别适用于单次学习或持续学习等问题场景，克服了传统直接条件作用范式的采样限制。

Abstract: Advancements in reinforcement learning have produced a variety of complex and useful intrinsic driving forces; crucially, these drivers operate under a direct conditioning paradigm. This form of conditioning limits our agents' capacity by restricting how they learn from the environment as well as from others. Off-policy or learn-by-example methods can learn from demonstrators' representations, but they require access to the demonstrating agent's policies or their reward functions. Our work overcomes this direct sampling limitation by introducing vicarious conditioning as an intrinsic reward mechanism. We draw from psychological and biological literature to provide a foundation for vicarious conditioning and use memory-based methods to implement its four steps: attention, retention, reproduction, and reinforcement. Crucially, our vicarious conditioning paradigms support low-shot learning and do not require the demonstrator agent's policy nor its reward functions. We evaluate our approach in the MiniWorld Sidewalk environment, one of the few public environments that features a non-descriptive terminal condition (no reward provided upon agent death), and extend it to Box2D's CarRacing environment. Our results across both environments demonstrate that vicarious conditioning enables longer episode lengths by discouraging the agent from non-descriptive terminal conditions and guiding the agent toward desirable states. Overall, this work emulates a cognitively-plausible learning paradigm better suited to problems such as single-life learning or continual learning.

</details>


### [140] [No More, No Less: Task Alignment in Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12233)
*Sina Mavali,David Pape,Jonathan Evertz,Samira Abedini,Devansh Srivastav,Thorsten Eisenhofer,Sahar Abdelnabi,Lea Schönherr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAB基准测试，包含89个终端任务，用于评估智能体在自然环境中选择性遵循相关指令、忽略干扰信息的能力。对10个前沿智能体的测试显示，任务完成能力与任务对齐能力之间存在系统性差距，现有防御机制无法区分相关线索与干扰信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法评估终端智能体是否具备选择性遵循环境指令的能力——智能体可能因盲目遵循所有指令而显得有能力，也可能因完全忽略指令而显得鲁棒，但两者均非真正的任务对齐。

Method: 从Terminal-Bench 2.1中构建89个任务，每个任务故意缺少关键信息，该信息嵌入在自然的环境工件（如README文件、代码注释、堆栈跟踪）中作为必要线索，同时放置一个似是而非的干扰信息。智能体需要选择性使用线索而忽略干扰才能完成任务。

Result: 最强的Terminal-Bench智能体任务完成率高但任务对齐率低。对6种提示注入防御的评估表明，抑制干扰执行的同时也抑制了完成任务所需的线索。

Conclusion: 任务对齐的智能体需要选择性使用环境指令，而非全盘接受或全盘拒绝。

Abstract: Terminal agents are increasingly capable of executing complex, long-horizon tasks autonomously from a single user prompt. To do so, they must interpret instructions encountered in the environment (e.g., README files, code comments, stack traces) and determine their relevance to the task. This creates a fundamental challenge: relevant cues must be followed to complete a task, whereas irrelevant or misleading ones must be ignored. Existing benchmarks do not capture this ability. An agent may appear capable by blindly following all instructions, or appear robust by ignoring them altogether. We introduce TAB (Task Alignment Benchmark), a suite of 89 terminal tasks derived from Terminal-Bench 2.1. Each task is intentionally underspecified, with missing information provided as a necessary cue embedded in a natural environmental artifact, alongside a plausible but irrelevant distractor. Solving these tasks requires selectively using the cue while ignoring the distractor. Applying TAB to ten frontier agents reveals a systematic gap between task capability and task alignment. The strongest Terminal-Bench agent achieves high task completion but low task alignment on TAB. Evaluating six prompt-injection defenses further shows that suppressing distractor execution also suppresses the cues required for task completion. These results demonstrate that task-aligned agents require selective use of environmental instructions rather than blanket acceptance or rejection.

</details>


### [141] [Instruction Lens Score: Your Instruction Contributes a Powerful Object Hallucination Detector for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12258)
*Runhe Lai,Xinhua Lu,Yanqi Wu,Jinlun Ye,Weijiang Yu,Ruixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Instruction Lens Score (InsLen)，通过分析指令令牌嵌入来检测多模态大语言模型中的对象幻觉问题，无需额外模型或训练，在多个基准测试中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中的对象幻觉问题严重阻碍了其可靠部署，现有检测方法存在依赖辅助模型或额外训练等局限性。

Method: 提出InsLen方法，包含两个核心组件：校准局部分数（Calibrated Local Score）和上下文一致性分数（Context Consistency Score），通过分析指令令牌嵌入中隐含的视觉信息来检测对象幻觉。

Result: 在多个基准测试和不同MLLM架构上的实验表明，InsLen持续优于现有的幻觉检测方法。

Conclusion: InsLen是一种即插即用的对象幻觉检测器，无需辅助模型或额外训练，有效且鲁棒。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, yet the object hallucination remains a critical challenge for reliable deployment. In this paper, we present an in-depth analysis of instruction token embeddings and reveal that they implicitly encode visual information while effectively filtering erroneous information introduced by misleading visual embeddings. Building on this insight, we propose the Instruction Lens Score (InsLen), which combines a Calibrated Local Score with a Context Consistency Score that measures context consistency of the object tokens. The proposed approach serves as a plug-and-play object hallucination detector without relying on auxiliary models or additional training. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and diverse MLLM architectures demonstrate that InsLen consistently outperforms existing hallucination detection methods, highlighting its effectiveness and robustness. The code is available at https://github.com/Fraserlairh/Instruction-Lens-Score.

</details>


### [142] [Delay-Empowered Causal Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12261)
*Chenran Zhao,Dianxi Shi,Haotian Wang,Mengzhu Wang,Yaowen Zhang,Chunping Qiu,Shaowu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DECHRL，一种通过因果建模和延迟感知赋能来处理强化学习中随机延迟的方法，在带随机延迟的2D-Minecraft和MiniGrid环境中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有延迟感知强化学习方法依赖状态增强、延迟先验知识或非延迟数据，泛化能力有限；层次强化学习的延时处理能力受限于固定延迟假设。

Method: 显式建模状态转移的因果结构及其关联的随机延迟分布，将其融入延迟感知的赋能目标，驱动主动探索以寻找高可控状态。

Result: 在修改后的2D-Minecraft和MiniGrid环境中，DECHRL有效建模了时间延迟，在时序不确定性下显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 因果层次结构与延迟感知赋能相结合能有效处理强化学习中的随机延迟问题。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks involve delayed effects, where the outcomes of actions emerge after varying time lags. Existing delay-aware reinforcement learning methods often rely on state augmentation, prior knowledge of delay distributions, or access to non-delayed data, limiting their generalization. Hierarchical reinforcement learning, by contrast, inherently offers advantages in handling delays due to its hierarchical structure, yet existing methods are restricted to fixed delays. To address these limitations, we propose Delay-Empowered Causal Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (DECHRL). DECHRL explicitly models both the causal structure of state transitions and their associated stochastic delay distributions. These are then incorporated into a delay-aware empowerment objective that drives proactive exploration toward highly controllable states, thereby improving performance under temporal uncertainty. We evaluate DECHRL in modified 2D-Minecraft and MiniGrid environments featuring stochastic delays. Experimental results show that DECHRL effectively models temporal delays and significantly outperforms baselines in decision-making under temporal uncertainty.

</details>


### [143] [PriorZero: Bridging Language Priors and World Models for Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12289)
*Junyu Xiong,Yuan Pu,Jia Tang,Yazhe Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PriorZero提出了一种解耦的 rollout-training 框架，将LLM概念先验融入基于世界模型的规划中。在rollout阶段通过根节点先验注入机制让MCTS聚焦语义合理的动作；在训练阶段通过交替优化解耦世界模型学习和LLM微调。在Jericho和BabyAI基准上提升了探索效率和渐近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的静态知识无法直接适应长任务复杂转移动态：将LLM先验作为固定策略限制了探索多样性，而端到端微调则面临优化不稳定和信用分配问题。需要弥合先验-动态不匹配这一根本差距。

Method: PriorZero包含两个核心设计：(1) 根节点先验注入机制——仅在MCTS根节点引入LLM先验，引导搜索朝向语义合理的动作，同时保留世界模型的深度前瞻能力；(2) 解耦的交替优化——世界模型持续在交互数据上优化以改进其动力学、策略和值预测，其值估计随后为LLM微调提供细粒度信用分配信号。

Result: 在文本冒险游戏（Jericho）和指令跟随网格世界任务（BabyAI）上，PriorZero一致性地提升了探索效率和渐近性能。

Conclusion: PriorZero建立了LLM赋能的决策制定的有前景框架，有效解决了先验-动态不匹配问题，将LLM的概念先验与基于世界模型的规划有机结合。

Abstract: Leveraging the rich world knowledge of Large Language Models (LLMs) to enhance Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents offers a promising path toward general intelligence. However, a fundamental prior-dynamics mismatch hinders existing approaches: static LLM knowledge cannot directly adapt to the complex transition dynamics of long-horizon tasks. Using LLM priors as fixed policies limits exploration diversity, as the prior is blind to environment-specific dynamics; while end-to-end fine-tuning suffers from optimization instability and credit assignment issues. To bridge this gap, we propose PriorZero, a unified framework that integrates LLM-derived conceptual priors into world-model-based planning through a decoupled rollout-training design. During rollout, a novel root-prior injection mechanism incorporates LLM priors exclusively at the root node of Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), focusing search on semantically promising actions while preserving the world model's deep lookahead capability. During training, PriorZero decouples world-model learning from LLM adaptation: the world model is continuously refined on interaction data to jointly improve its dynamics, policy, and value predictions, its value estimates are then leveraged to provide fine-grained credit assignment signals for stable LLM fine-tuning via alternating optimization. Experiments across diverse benchmarks, including text-based adventure games in Jericho and instruction-following gridworld tasks in BabyAI, demonstrate that PriorZero consistently improves both exploration efficiency and asymptotic performance, establishing a promising framework for LLM-empowered decision-making. Our code is available at https://github.com/opendilab/LightZero.

</details>


### [144] [Targeted Neuron Modulation via Contrastive Pair Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12290)
*Sam Herring,Jake Naviasky,Karan Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比神经元归因（CNA）方法，通过识别MLP层中区分有害与良性提示的关键神经元（占0.1%），在不依赖梯度或辅助训练的情况下实现高效模型行为干预。实验表明，在指令微调模型中消融该电路可降低50%以上的拒绝率且保持输出质量，而基础模型虽存在类似结构但干预仅产生内容偏移而非行为改变。研究发现对齐微调将预存在的判别结构转化为稀疏可定向的拒绝门控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差流干预方法在高强度干预时会导致输出连贯性退化，限制了实用场景中的应用。同时，语言模型指令微调后拒绝有害请求的底层机制尚不明确，缺乏精准的神经元级理解与操控手段。

Method: 提出对比神经元归因（CNA），通过前向传播识别MLP神经元中区分有害与良性提示的激活差异，筛选出0.1%的关键神经元进行消融干预。跨Llama和Qwen架构（1B-72B参数）对比基础模型与指令微调模型。

Result: 指令微调模型中消融该电路可降低50%以上拒绝率，且在所有干预强度下保持流畅性和非退化性。基础模型存在类似结构但干预仅导致内容偏移，无法实现行为改变。

Conclusion: 神经元级干预可在不牺牲输出质量的前提下实现可靠的行为导向控制；对齐微调将基础模型中已有的判别结构转化为稀疏、可定向的拒绝门控机制。

Abstract: Language models are instruction-tuned to refuse harmful requests, but the mechanisms underlying this behavior remain poorly understood. Popular steering methods operate on the residual stream and degrade output coherence at high intervention strengths, limiting their practical use. We introduce contrastive neuron attribution (CNA), which identifies the 0.1% of MLP neurons whose activations most distinguish harmful from benign prompts, requiring only forward passes with no gradients or auxiliary training. In instruct models, ablating the discovered circuit reduces refusal rates by over 50% on a standard jailbreak benchmark while preserving fluency and non-degeneracy across all steering strengths. Applying CNA to matched base and instruct models across Llama and Qwen architectures (from 1B to 72B parameters), we find that base models contain similar late-layer discrimination structures but steering these neurons produces only content shifts, not behavioral change. These results demonstrate that neuron-level intervention enables reliable behavioral steering without the quality tradeoffs of residual-stream methods. More broadly, our findings suggest that alignment fine-tuning transforms pre-existing discrimination structure into a sparse, targetable refusal gate.

</details>


### [145] [STRABLE: Benchmarking Tabular Machine Learning with Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12292)
*Gioia Blayer,Myung Jun Kim,Félix Lefebvre,Lennart Purucker,Alan Arazi,Eilam Shapira,Roi Reichart,Frank Hutter,Marine Le Morvan,David Holzmüller,Gaël Varoquaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了STRABLE基准测试集，包含108个真实世界包含字符串和数值的表格数据集，通过评估445个管道，系统研究了在表格学习中处理字符串条目的方法，发现大多数表格以类别型为主，简单字符串嵌入配合高级表格学习器即可高效预测；在自由文本主导的表格中，大型LLM编码器更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界表格常包含字符串条目，但现有基准测试集多聚焦于纯数值表格，对包含字符串的表格学习研究不足，缺乏系统的基准测试平台来研究是否需要端到端的专用架构，或仅需将字符串编码为数值即可。

Method: 构建了STRABLE基准测试集（108个真实表格），评估了445个管道，包括端到端架构和模块化管道（字符串编码→后处理→表格学习器），对比了不同字符串编码方法和表格学习器的组合效果。

Result: 多数表格为类别主导型，简单字符串嵌入+高级表格学习器即可低计算成本取得好效果；在自由文本主导表格中，大型LLM编码器更具竞争力；不同LLM家族对后处理敏感度不同；STRABLE的管道排名接近理想排名。

Conclusion: STRABLE可作为包含字符串的表格学习研究的坚实基础，揭示了不同场景下字符串编码策略与表格学习器的选择指南。

Abstract: Benchmarking tabular learning has revealed the benefit of dedicated architectures, pushing the state of the art. But real-world tables often contain string entries, beyond numbers, and these settings have been understudied due to a lack of a solid benchmarking suite. They lead to new research questions: Are dedicated learners needed, with end-to-end modeling of strings and numbers? Or does it suffice to encode strings as numbers, as with a categorical encoding? And if so, do the resulting tables resemble numerical tabular data, calling for the same learners? To enable these studies, we contribute STRABLE, a benchmarking corpus of 108 tables, all real-world learning problems with strings and numbers across diverse application fields. We run the first large-scale empirical study of tabular learning with strings, evaluating 445 pipelines. These pipelines span end-to-end architectures and modular pipelines, where strings are first encoded, then post-processed, and finally passed to a tabular learner. We find that, because most tables in the wild are categorical-dominant, advanced tabular learners paired with simple string embeddings achieve good predictions at low computational cost. On free-text-dominant tables, large LLM encoders become competitive. Their performance also appears sensitive to post-processing, with differences across LLM families. Finally, we show that STRABLE is a good set of tables to study "string tabular" learning as it leads to generalizable pipeline rankings that are close to the oracle rankings. We thus establish STRABLE as a foundation for research on tabular learning with strings, an important yet understudied area.

</details>


### [146] [Approximation of Maximally Monotone Operators : A Graph Convergence Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12301)
*Takashi Furuya,Yury Korolev,Takaharu Yaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用图收敛（Painlevé-Kuratowski收敛）框架来近似不连续或集值算子（如微分算子），证明了传统的一致和L^p逼近在此类问题上存在根本性不足，并针对极大单调算子证明了可通过连续编码器-解码器架构实现局部图收敛逼近，进一步通过基于预解式的参数化构建了保持极大单调性的结构保持逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子学习在连续无限维空间映射（如PDE解算子）上取得了成功，但许多重要算子（包括微分算子）是不连续或集值的，超出了经典近似框架的适用范围，亟需新的范式来解决这类算子的逼近问题。

Method: 将问题重新形式化为基于图收敛（Painlevé-Kuratowski收敛）的逼近框架，该框架适用于闭算子。针对极大单调算子，采用连续编码器-解码器架构证明局部图收敛下的逼近可行性，并利用预解式参数化构建保持极大单调性的结构保持逼近。

Result: 证明了传统的一致和L^p逼近在不连续/集值算子场景下存在根本性不足；证明了任意极大单调算子可在局部图收敛意义下被连续编码器-解码器架构逼近；进一步构造了通过预解式参数化的结构保持逼近方法。

Conclusion: 图收敛框架为不连续/集值算子的学习提供了新的理论范式，弥补了经典逼近方法的不足，并展示了在保持算子结构性质（如极大单调性）方面的可行性。

Abstract: Operator learning has been highly successful for continuous mappings between infinite-dimensional spaces, such as PDE solution operators. However, many operators of interest-including differential operators-are discontinuous or set-valued, and lie outside classical approximation frameworks. We propose a paradigm shift by formulating approximation via graph convergence (Painlevé-Kuratowski convergence), which is well-suited for closed operators. We show that uniform and $L^p$ approximation are fundamentally inadequate in this setting. Focusing on maximally monotone operators, we prove that any such operator can be approximated in the sense of local graph convergence by continuous encoder-decoder architectures, and further construct structure-preserving approximations that retain maximal monotonicity via resolvent-based parameterizations.

</details>


### [147] [Transferable Delay-Aware Reinforcement Learning via Implicit Causal Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12312)
*Chenran Zhao,Dianxi Shi,Yaowen Zhang,Chunping Qiu,Shaowu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐式因果图建模的可迁移延迟感知强化学习方法，通过场节点编码器将高维观测表示为具有节点级语义的潜状态，并利用消息传递机制建模动态因果依赖，在潜空间中进行想象驱动的行为学习与规划，从而在随机延迟和跨任务场景下实现知识迁移与快速策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机延迟会削弱动作与状态反馈之间的时序对应关系，使智能体难以识别动作效应的真实传播过程；而跨任务场景中任务目标和奖励公式的变化进一步降低了先前任务知识的可复用性。

Method: 提出基于隐式因果图建模的可迁移延迟感知强化学习方法：使用场节点编码器将高维观测表示为具有节点级语义的潜状态，通过消息传递机制刻画节点间的动态因果依赖，学习可迁移的结构化表示和环境动力学知识；在此基础上结合想象驱动的行为学习与规划，在潜空间中优化策略。

Result: 在带有随机延迟的DMC连续控制任务上，该方法优于基线方法；跨任务迁移实验表明，所学结构化表示和动力学知识可有效迁移至新任务，并显著加速策略适应。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效应对随机延迟带来的挑战，并通过结构化表示和动力学知识的跨任务迁移实现快速策略适应，提升了强化学习在复杂环境下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Random delays weaken the temporal correspondence between actions and subsequent state feedback, making it difficult for agents to identify the true propagation process of action effects. In cross-task scenarios, changes in task objectives and reward formulations further reduce the reusability of previously acquired task knowledge. To address this problem, this paper proposes a transferable delay-aware reinforcement learning method based on implicit causal graph modeling. The proposed method uses a field-node encoder to represent high-dimensional observations as latent states with node-level semantics, and employs a message-passing mechanism to characterize dynamic causal dependencies among nodes, thereby learning transferable structured representations and environment dynamics knowledge. On this basis, imagination-driven behavior learning and planning are incorporated to optimize policies in the latent space, enabling cross-task knowledge transfer and rapid adaptation. Experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms baseline methods on DMC continuous control tasks with random delays.
